Isolated analyst agents produce truly independent analyses, cross-examine each other's work, and every attack is verified by an independent adjudicator before anyone has to answer it. Convergence is measured, not self-declared; confidence is computed from signals, not vibes. Use for irreversible decisions, complex strategy, or any question where the cost of being wrong is high. ([sturlese/agent-challenge](https://github.com/sturlese/agent-challenge/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
- **crucible**: Vertical-slice test-driven development for prose claims. Hardens a thesis against adversarial objections one cycle at a time using adversary, proponent, and judge subagents; produces a regression-checkable transcript of which objections the thesis has survived. Use when the user says "crucible this thesis", "put this through the crucible", "engage crucible", "harden this argument", "stress-test this claim", "tdd this argument", or wants to subject a load-bearing technical, strategic, policy, or philosophical claim to the same RED/GREEN/refactor discipline as smart-mode's Phase 3. ([trevsm/skills](https://github.com/trevsm/skills/tree/HEAD/crucible))
- **crucible**: Put a change through rounds of adversarial review by a reviewer that is not the model doing the work, fixing real findings and declining the rest on a strict budget. Use when the user says "crucible", asks to harden, stress or adversarially review a change, or wants a review loop before filing a PR. ([Udhay-Adithya/skills](https://github.com/Udhay-Adithya/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/crucible))
